FOC Web -- Sweep 1, the heroes: PARTIAL RESULT (13 of 26)
========================================================

Generated 2026-08-31 by the fan-out described in SweepPlan.TXT: one read-only
agent per hero over every ability that hero can reach, then every non-CORRECT
verdict re-checked by two further agents that had not seen the finder's
reasoning, prompted to REFUTE it and defaulting to refuted when uncertain.

  >>> COVERAGE IS HALF THE ROSTER. READ "WHAT DID NOT RUN" BELOW BEFORE
  >>> TREATING ANY ABSENCE HERE AS AN ALL-CLEAR.

THIS IS A QUEUE OF CANDIDATES, NOT CONCLUSIONS. SweepPlan.TXT is explicit that
the sweep does not write code and does not write TODO.txt -- TODO.txt takes
only what has been verified and measured. A row here has survived two hostile
readers, which is not the same as having been reproduced in play. Confirm
before fixing.

Found by the same pass but NOT in this file, because they are in the half of
the roster that never ran or in a base no shard reached:
  Akiha A04T (AUim)   Impale's slots are Wave Distance / Wave Time / Damage /
                      Air Time; read as Carrion Swarm it ran a 2000-long,
                      1-wide line capped at 0.3 damage and hit nothing.
  AUfn                Frost Nova's primary and splash damage were reversed
                      (Ufn2 is the specific-target damage). Latent -- A06J
                      zeroes both.
  AEsf, ANcl          Starfall was sharing Blizzard's fields, which
                      AbilityMetaData never gives it; ANcl was aliased to Chain
                      Lightning and is not Chain Lightning. Both unused by this
                      map; both corrected so they stay that way.

The "phantom d1 || N default" pattern at the top of this file is also fixed, at
its root rather than row by row. `execute` now keeps a data slot undefined when
the map set nothing and reads it through `slot(v, default)`, so an explicit 0
survives instead of being replaced by 80 or 100. Measured over all 1046
compiled abilities, 27 of the map's own ids carry an explicit all-level zero at
a site the engine defaulted over. This file names 18 of them; 9 it never saw.
Of the 14 rows it lists under that pattern, 11 are closed by this -- A02V,
A03L, A007, A044, A042, A05V, A062, A06J, A04I, A04R and A04N -- plus the
contested A006, A048, A059 and A06K. A01N and A01G are closed by the routing
fixes above. Gilgamesh A03P is NOT closed: AEer has no engine case at all, so
it is a different defect and stays open.

Also closed: the AOws case floored a 0.01 duration to a full second, stunning
for 초콜릿이 되라, 적주함발, 드래곤회오리 치기 and 만뇌 where the map had
removed the stun.

The instrument that found the routing and slot defects is tools/slot_test.mjs,
and it covers the whole roster rather than the 13 heroes below -- so for that
class, the uncovered half is no longer uncovered.

WHAT DID NOT RUN
----------------
Sweep 1 was split into two halves of 13 heroes because 26 finders plus their
verifiers exceeds the session agent cap -- the split SweepPlan.TXT itself
prescribes. Half 1 completed: 39 of 39 agents, 0 errors. Half 2 was launched
and every one of its 13 finder agents failed on the same error, "You have hit
your monthly spend limit", before doing any work. It produced 0 rows. Nothing
was salvageable and it was not retried.

  Covered (13):
      마인부우 (Majin Buu), InuYasha, Sandaime Hokage, Orochimaru, Eneru,
      Yusuke Urameshi, Gilgamesh, Akiha Tohno, Renji Abarai, Nico Robin,
      Rock Lee, Monkey D. Luffy, Haku

  NOT COVERED (13, no data of any kind was collected on these):
      Shiki Tohno, Gaara, Uryu Ishida, Portgas D. Ace, Kisame Hoshigaki,
      Kimimaro Kaguya, Itachi Uchiha, Alucard, Roronoa Zoro, Rob Lucci
      with Hattori, Ichigo Kurosaki, Goku, Byakuya Kuchiki

AbilityAudit.txt reports three abilities diagnosed from play. Two of the three
sit in the uncovered half -- Kisame A069 and Byakuya A00T; only Orochimaru
A01K was covered here. Gaara A03O, which shares A069's unimplemented
TerrainDeform crater native, is also uncovered. The uncovered half is not the
easy half.


SCOPE AND METHOD
----------------
The full roster is 26 heroes, 125 castable slots, 180 reachable ability rows.
This file covers 13 heroes and 90 rows. Reachability is the rule
tools/ability_audit.mjs uses: each hero's castables, plus transitively the
abilities of any unit type those name in `unit` -- which is both the morph
forms and the summons. The manifest for the covered 13 held 89 rows; 90 came
back, because the Buu shard added A02Z, the chained ability its own trigger
casts. That is extra coverage, not a miscount.

Each finder was required to quote the raw field code every number came from
(data/war3map.w3a.json mods, war3_extracted/Units/AbilityData.slk, with data-
slot meanings resolved through AbilityMetaData.slk), to name file:line for
every claim about server/ or client/, and to quote the command it actually
ran. A figure present in data/abilities.json but in no w3a mod and no SLK row
is flagged as a compiler artifact. UNESTABLISHABLE was offered as a first-
class verdict; no shard used it, which is itself worth a sceptical look.

The three verifier lenses, rotated in pairs across the roster so the check is
diverse rather than three copies of one: (1) the number vs the raw field, (2)
does the cited code path actually execute, (3) would a player notice. Every
non-CORRECT row got two of the three. A row is listed as SURVIVED only if BOTH
lenses declined to refute it.

COUNTS (covered half only)
--------------------------
  rows                      90
  CORRECT                   11
  APPROXIMATE               52
  DEAD                      27
  UNESTABLISHABLE           0

  non-CORRECT               79
  survived both lenses      53   (high 23, medium 28, low 2)
  refuted or split          26   <- NOT actionable as written; see the tail

26 of 79 findings did not survive. That is the single most useful number in
this file: it is the rate at which a confident, well-evidenced agent finding
fails a second reader, and it is why the verify half was not ceremony.


RECURRING PATTERNS
------------------
Grouped after the fact by searching each row's own report text for the
signature below -- so this is a coarse textual index, not a verified claim
that every listed row shares one root cause. It is here because several rows
plainly do, and one edit would move several at once. "surv" counts only rows
that survived both lenses; the rest mention the pattern somewhere in their
text, including inside a refutation.

  phantom `d1 || N` default overriding a damage the map deliberately zeroed
      18 rows mention it, 14 survived both lenses
      surviving: 마인부우 A02V, InuYasha A01N, InuYasha A03L, Sandaime
          Hokage A01G, Gilgamesh A007, Gilgamesh A03P, Akiha Tohno A044,
          Akiha Tohno A042, Renji Abarai A05V, Renji Abarai A062, Rock
          Lee A06J, Rock Lee A04I, Monkey D. Luffy A04R, Monkey D. Luffy
          A04N

  dummy units never removed -- no timed life, no RemoveUnit, keep attacking
      10 rows mention it, 8 survived both lenses
      surviving: InuYasha A03K, Yusuke Urameshi A00N, Gilgamesh A03P,
          Akiha Tohno A042, Renji Abarai A065, Renji Abarai A062, Nico
          Robin A06O, Nico Robin A06S

  buff named by the data but never stamped (applyBuff called without a code)
      8 rows mention it, 8 survived both lenses
      surviving: 마인부우 A02V, InuYasha A040, InuYasha A03K, Eneru A020,
          Eneru A01X, Akiha Tohno A04F, Haku A05C, Haku Afzy

  morph-granted ability has no command-card slot, so it is uncastable
      5 rows mention it, 3 survived both lenses
      surviving: Eneru A01Y, Monkey D. Luffy A04Y, Haku A05C

  Blizzard data slot read positionally: abilities.js:270-271 swaps Hbz1/Hbz2
      2 rows mention it, 2 survived both lenses
      surviving: Gilgamesh A027, Nico Robin A06R

  blink distance uses i.range (SLK Rng1=99999) instead of Ebl1 (abilities.js:258)
      3 rows mention it, 2 survived both lenses
      surviving: Eneru A01U, Renji Abarai A04M

  ANms mana shield / damage absorption has no implementation anywhere
      3 rows mention it, 2 survived both lenses
      surviving: Sandaime Hokage A00S, Nico Robin A06S

1. [HIGH] Akiha Tohno  A04F (AHfs)
      달을 뚫는다 (Flame Strike)
      APPROXIMATE -- SURVIVED both lenses

      Flame Strike's real data survives (15xlvl per 0.2s plus 10xlvl per 1s
      over 10s, area 200-425, capped at 300xlvl) but case 'AHfs' deals data1
      once to a single unit -- 150 of 3000 at level 10 -- and returns 'need
      target' on a ground click, so a point cast deals nothing at all.

      numbers: Hfs1:1 absent -> DataA1=15 (AbilityData.slk AHfs); Hfs1:2..10=30..150
      (w3a) = "Full Damage Dealt"; Hfs2:1..10=0.2 = "Full Damage
      Interval"; Hfs3:1..10=10..100 = "Half Damage Dealt"; Hfs4/Hfs5
      absent -> DataD=1 ("Half Damage Interval") / DataE=0.75 ("Building
      Reduction"); Hfs6:1..10=300..3000 = "Maximum Damage"; aare:1
      absent -> Area1=200, aare:2..10=225..425; adur=10.0; ahdu=4.01;
      acdn=12.0; amcs=0; acas=0; alev:0=10; alsk:0=5; arlv absent -> SLK
      reqLevel=1; range 800 = Rng1..3; buff BHfs = BuffID1.
      delivery: engine case -- server/abilities.js:354-360 (case
      'ANdr'/'AUdc'/'AHfs'/'ANin'), reached from
      server/world.js:1489-1491 (no missile art, so runAbility runs at
      cast time)
      fallback: Not a fallback, but the engine case is fallback-grade: it is the shared
      single-target-nuke case shared with Death Coil and Drain, and it
      reads only data1. The base's real mechanic is a burning ground --
      Hfs2/Hfs3/Hfs4 (interval, half damage, half interval), the 10s
      adur and the Hfs6 damage cap are all present in the data and none
      is read anywhere in server/. At level 10 the map's own numbers cap
      at 3000 over 10 seconds in a 425 radius; the port deals 150 to one
      unit. This is the A03T pattern.
      script: One reference, at byte 236001 of extracted/war3map.j: `function Tj ...
      return(GetSpellAbilityId()=='A04F')`, action `uj`, which loops 4
      times creating unit 'h018' at PolarProjection(GetSpellTargetLoc(),
      250, facing+90*i). 'h018' occurs exactly once in the script (count
      command below), carries only Aloc in data/unittypes.json, is never
      given an ability and never ordered -- it is decoration
      (DoomDeath.mdl, hp 2, hpReg -1). So the map adds no damage here:
      all of A04F's damage is meant to be the base Flame Strike. Trigger
      registered on EVENT_PLAYER_UNIT_SPELL_EFFECT (udg_trigger185),
      fired by server/world.js:1496-1501.
      buffs: BHfs (SLK BuffID1..4; no abuf override). The script never names 'BHfs'
      (count = 0). Nothing applies it as a code:
      server/abilities.js:354-360 calls w.damage only, never
      w.applyBuff, so the BHfs entry in data/buffart.json
      (FlameStrikeDamageTarget.mdl, count 1, point "chest") never hangs
      on anything -- server/world.js:2071 only sends buff art for buffs
      carrying a `code`.
      art: special = Specialart FlameStrike1/2/FlameStrike.mdl
      (HumanAbilityFunc.txt AHfs, not overridden); effect = aeat:0 =
      MarkOfChaosTarget.mdl (map override of retail
      FlameStrikeTarget.mdl); anim = Animnames "spell,channel";
      target/count/points inherited from buff BHfs. All five models
      exist under assets/models and are indexed in
      public/assets/models.json; server/world.js:1826-1852
      (emitAbilityArt) emits sfx/sfxUnit for
      caster/target/special/area/effect and client/js/main.js:289-290
      renders them. flamestrike1.glb carries 5 w3particle emitters and
      markofchaostarget.glb 6, so they draw. Art reaches the client.
      sound: None declared. The w3a entry has no sound field and data/abilities.json
      carries no sound key at all -- 0 of 1046 entries (key census
      command below). Ability EffectSound is never extracted, so no
      ability sound reaches the client for any spell in the map.
      compiler artifacts: None. Every figure traces to a w3a mod or an AbilityData.slk cell.
      Levels 4-10 clone SLK level 3 (tools/abilities.py:297-299), which
      is what the World Editor does, so range/data4/data5 at those
      levels are inherited, not invented.
      confirmed: Reached. `grep -n "AHfs" server/*.js` = only abilities.js:56,354 (no
      burning ground). A04F art has no missile so world.js:1798 returns
      false and 1490 runs case AHfs. Both halves live: main.js:509-516
      sends targetId on a unit click even in point mode, else 'need

4. [HIGH] Gilgamesh  A007 (AUcs)
      게이트 오브 바빌론 / Gate of Babylon
      APPROXIMATE -- SURVIVED both lenses

      The map zeroes every AUcs field so the base does nothing, but
      server/abilities.js:200-202 substitutes `d1 || 100`, `i.range` and `||
      120` and fires an invented 100-damage 700x120 line; the real payload
      (six ANst dummies) then lands as ~29 damage total because the fallback
      reads Nst1 "Beasts Per Second" as damage.

      numbers: Compiled L1-3 are ALL ZERO and that is the map's own intent:
      Ucs1:1..3=0.0 (Ucs1 = "Damage", AbilityMetaData.slk ID=Ucs1
      field=Data data=1), Ucs2:1..3=0.0 ("Max Damage"), Ucs3:1..3=0.0
      ("Distance"), Ucs4:1..3=0.0 ("Final Area"), aare:1..3=0.0.
      acdn:1..3=100.0; amcs:1..3=0; range 700 and levels=3 are
      unoverridden SLK (AbilityData.slk AUcs Rng1=700, levels=3). The
      w3a also carries Ucs1:4..7=1000/1200/1500/1750, but alev is not
      set so the ability has 3 levels and rows 4-7 are dead in the map
      too. Real payload is on the dummies: A02B Nst1:1=8 / Nst3:1=100 /
      Nst4:1=50 / adur:1=15.0; A02O 4/150/80; A029 6/200/100; A02A
      4/300/50; A02P 3/450/100; A028 4/300/300 (Nst1="Beasts Per
      Second", Nst3="Damage Amount", Nst4="Damage Radius").
      delivery: engine case -- server/abilities.js:196-214 (case 'AUcs'); the six
      dummies reach server/world.js:811 -> castDummy
      server/world.js:1411 -> runAbility server/world.js:1406 -> the
      default fallback at server/abilities.js:379-391
      fallback: The dummy half is a fallback and does NOT resemble Stampede:
      server/abilities.js:386-388 damages everything within `area` once
      for `d1`. d1 is Nst1 "Beasts Per Second" (8/4/6/4/3/4), not
      damage, and the radius used is aare=400 rather than Nst4=50..300.
      The six dummies therefore deliver ~29 damage total in six instant
      400-radius pulses centred 100 units in front of the caster, where
      the map asks for six 15-second stampedes doing 100-450 per beast
      impact. This is the A03T failure mode a second time, with a
      positional data-slot misread on top.
      script: 4 hits at bytes 197510, 197606, 197723, 197840. Condition Th (=='A007')
      on udg_trigger144, EVENT_PLAYER_UNIT_SPELL_EFFECT, action Wh. Wh
      plays udg_sound36, creates one h00V portal 400 units behind the
      caster, then by level creates 2 / 4 / 6 dummies (h00X,h00W |
      +h00Y,h00Z | +h010,h011) and issues each

CONTESTED -- at least one lens refuted the finding

  Eneru                    A01W (AEev)  맨트라 (Mantra / Evasion)
        Matches the map: 25% evade is delivered. Two corrections to
        AbilityAudit.txt lines 71-72, which lists this as dead — AEev is now
        handled, and A01W is on the BASE hero too (unittypes.json
        O002.abilities), not only on the morph form.
  Gilgamesh                A02C (AOsh)  에누마 엘리쉬 / Enuma Elish
        Matches the map: the line case reads Osh1/Osh2/Osh3/Osh4 in their real
        meanings (4000-6000 damage, 1e9 cap, 2000 length, 400 width) and the
        script adds only visuals. Residual: a missile model the map deleted
        still draws, and the 1 s acas cast time is not honoured.
  InuYasha                 A023 (AHtb)  산혼철조 (Sankon Tesso)
        Matches the map: damage, cooldown, mana and the trigger's teleport all
        land. One drift — abilities.js:141 takes heroDuration in preference,
        so a non-hero is stunned 2s/3s instead of the SLK's 5s at ranks 4-5.
  Monkey D. Luffy          AInv (AInv)  Inventory
        Matches the map by coincidence rather than by reading it: the six
        slots the ability declares are hardcoded in world.js:955, so a unit
        that does not carry AInv would still get six slots.
  Orochimaru               A01H (ANsg)  소환술-잠영사수 / Hidden Shadow Snake Hands
        Matches the map: 2/4/4/6/6 snakes of the right type for 30 s on a 60 s
        cooldown. Only invention is the spawn ring radius, taken from `area`
        (200) at server/abilities.js:115 rather than from any field.
  Orochimaru               A01K (AOre)  금술-불로불사의 술법 / Forbidden Jutsu: Living Corpse Reincarnation
        The mechanic and both numbers match, but A01K sits in the hero's
        castable slot list (game.json castable index 4) and castAbility
        charges its 120 s cooldown before execute rejects it as passive — one
        click on the icon disables the revive for two minutes.
  Orochimaru               A024 (ACs8)  소환술-만다 / Summoning Jutsu: Manda
        Right answer from the wrong field: the AOsf/ACs8 family keeps its
        count in Osf2 = DataB (compiled data2), but summonFrom reads i.data1,
        which is absent here, so n falls back to 1 — which happens to equal
        Osf2:1..5=1. Latent for any AOsf ability with a count above one.
  Rock Lee                 A04G (AEme)  팔문둔갑 / Eight Gates
        Matches the map: morphs into H01G for 15/25/35/45/60 s on a 100 s
        cooldown, and the form's faster attack (atkCd 0.5 vs 1.5) and movement
        (400 vs 250) deliver the tooltip's attack- and move-speed increase.
  Rock Lee                 AInv (AInv)  Inventory
        Matches the map in outcome — 6 inventory slots — but only because the
        count is hardcoded rather than read from DataA1; data/game.json also
        reports hasInventory:false for H01D (tools/compile_game.py:269 reads
        only the hero's own w3u uabi, which H01D does not set), a value
        nothing in client/ or server/ actually consumes.
  Yusuke Urameshi          A025 (AHtb)  영광탄 (Spirit Gun finishing strike)
        Damage 2500/5000/7500 and the 1s stun both match Htb1 and adur/ahdu
        and the author's tooltips (STRING 670-672). The only defect is the
        compiled buff BPSE surviving an explicit blank override, which is
        inert because the engine's stun carries no buff code.
  Yusuke Urameshi          A026 (Aamk)  마족대격세 (Demon-clan awakening)
        Matches the map: +50 AGI, +50 STR, +0 INT via ATTR_SKILLS, and
        GetHeroStr/GetHeroAgi return the totals so it counts toward the
        250/250 awakening threshold. The tooltip (STRING 682/684) claims '모든

==============================================================================
HOW TO FINISH THE OTHER HALF
==============================================================================

Nothing needs rebuilding. The generator, the per-hero manifest and the
workflow script are all still on disk, and the half-2 run failed before any
agent produced output, so there is no cache to salvage and no partial state to
reconcile -- it is a clean re-launch of 13 finders plus their verifiers (39
agents) once spend allows.

  Artifacts, saved next to this file so nothing depends on the temp dir:
      HeroSweep.half1.json      raw workflow return value, all 90 rows with
                                every verifier verdict, unabridged
      tools/sweep_manifest.mjs  regenerates the 26-hero / 180-row work list
      tools/sweep1.workflow.mjs the workflow script (prompts, schemas, lenses)
      tools/write_sweep.py      regenerates this file from the raw JSON

  Half 1 run id: wf_3e01000d-b10   Half 2 (failed) run id: wf_1bb3aa56-e5b

To render this file again from the raw JSON, including after a half-2 run
lands: python3 tools/write_sweep.py <half1.json> [<half2.json>] HeroSweep.TXT
-- it takes any number of workflow outputs and merges them.


==============================================================================
WHAT THE SWEEP ITSELF TAUGHT
==============================================================================

  The verify half is the half that matters.
      26 of 79 findings -- every one of them written with a file:line and a
      quoted command -- failed a second reader who had not seen the reasoning.
      Finders are confident in exactly the same tone whether they are right or
      wrong.

  ability_audit.mjs cannot see any of this by construction.
      It asks whether an engine case exists. Nearly every row here is a case
      that exists and is wrong -- a slot read positionally, a default
      substituted over the map's own zero, a range taken from the order
      instead of the ability. All of it reports green structurally.

  Data slots must be resolved through AbilityMetaData.slk, never positionally.
      The Byakuya petal-count-as-damage bug already in AbilityAudit.txt is not
      a one-off; the same class turned up three more times in 13 heroes.
      Requiring every number to be quoted with its raw field code is what
      surfaced them.

  The compiled table is not the map.
      data/abilities.json carries values the w3a contradicts --
      tools/abilities.py:219 uses text(), and text('') returns None at
      tools/abilities.py:34-38, so the map's own acat:0="" (art cleared) is
      skipped rather than applied. Auditing against the compiled table alone
      would have called that correct.

  No shard returned UNESTABLISHABLE.
      It was offered twice in every prompt as a first-class answer and used
      zero times in 90 rows. Either the data really does settle every
      question, or the pull toward a definite answer beat the instruction.
      Worth assuming the latter when reading any single row here.

  Cost is the binding constraint, not correctness.
      Half 1 spent ~5.0M subagent tokens over 2099 tool calls for 13 heroes.
      Half 2 hit the monthly limit and returned nothing for ~1.6M tokens of
      partial work. Budget the second half as its own run rather than assuming
      a 26-hero sweep fits in one.
